## 1. Aria golden blast radius

**Harness** — `captureStableAria` at `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/apps/web/tests/scaffold.ts:947`. Critically it uses `page.locator(selector).first()` (scaffold.ts:948) and polls until two normalized captures match. `compareOrRefreshGolden` at scaffold.ts:967; `assertFixtureInventory` at scaffold.ts:986. Lane config: `vitest.web.config.ts:26-34` (include `apps/web/tests/**/*.e2e.ts` + `*.snapshot.ts`, `fileParallelism: false`).

**105 call sites across 49 e2e specs.** Complete selector inventory by width:

| Width | Selector | Sites |
|---|---|---|
| Whole Window | `[data-desktop-window]` | `desktop-recovery.e2e.ts:43` (WINDOW) → used at :147, :163, :183, :201 |
| Whole ChatSurface | `[class*="frame"]` | `lifecycle-chrome.e2e.ts:122, :172` only |
| Conversation column | `[class*="centerCol"]` | 40 sites in 24 specs |
| Region | `[class*="listArea"]` `[class*="viewArea"]` (navigation-panes:211, :285), `[class*="titleRow"]` `[class*="heroWorkspaceRow"]` (agent-preset-selection:280, :200), `[class*="menu"]` (background-job-list:109, :124) | 6 |
| Roles | `[role="dialog"]` (~20), `[role="listbox"]` (5), `[role="menu"]` (2), `[role="tree"][aria-label="Sessions"]` (cold-blank-session:56, message-actions:189, subagent-conversation:349/:467, sidebar-subagent-activity:144), `[role="tree"][aria-label="Subagent sessions"]` (subagent-conversation:290, :327, :411), `[role="treeitem"][aria-selected="true"]` (plan-review:93, question-composer:116) | ~32 |
| data-hooks | `[data-terminal]` (pwsh-terminal:94, navigation-panes:504), `[data-goal-bar]` (goal-bar:53), `[data-approval-key]`, `[data-question-key]`, `[data-plan-review-key]`, `[data-workflow-run]`, `[data-chat-flow]`, `[data-history-load]`, `[data-schedule-reminder]`, `[data-state="ongoing"]`, `[data-plugin-entry$="ui-settings"]` (settings-chrome:29) | ~14 |

**No selector is body-, html-, or root-level, and none captures the desktop frame.** `DesktopFrame.module.css` locals are `.desktop` / `.window` / `.overlayLayer` — none contains "frame", so `[class*="frame"]` resolves to `ChatSurface.module.css .frame`, and `[class*="centerCol"]` is only in `packages/client/ui-chat-surface/src/client/ChatSurface.module.css`.

**A taskbar/launcher in the shell chrome:**
- Rendered as a sibling of the Window wrappers inside `.desktop`, or into the `shell.overlay` list slot (`packages/client/ui-desktop/src/client/DesktopFrame.tsx:122-124`, declared at `packages/client/ui-desktop/src/client/index.ts:70,117`, and explicitly recommended by `packages/client/runtime/src/client/slots.ts:31-42`): **it enters zero existing goldens.** Every capture is at or below `[data-desktop-window]`.
- The two desktop-recovery goldens (`apps/web/tests/snapshots/desktop-recovery/window.expected.md`, `recovery.expected.md`) change only if the chrome lands *inside* the Window wrapper. `window.expected.md` today holds the whole ChatSurface (sidebar, hero, composer, Settings).
- `[role="dialog"]` goldens are immune regardless — primitives portal to `document.body` (`Modal.tsx:55`, `Menu.tsx:299`, `HoverCard.tsx:213`, `Toast.tsx:52`) or `#root` (`OnboardingSurface.tsx:22-28`).

**The real hazard is a second window, not the taskbar.** `.first()` makes all 40 `centerCol` captures and both `[class*="frame"]` captures silently bind to the DOM-first ChatSurface. Also count-based: `desktop-recovery.e2e.ts:182` `expect(await page.locator(CHAT_FRAME).count()).toBe(0)`. Also `.first()` on shared locators: `details-session-lifecycle.e2e.ts:42` (`[style*="grid-template-columns"]`), `page.locator('textarea').first()` across ~30 specs, and `waitForSelector('[class*="frame"]')` as the boot barrier in nearly every spec.

**Session open/close flows assuming single-window navigation** (one line each):
- `seeded-history.e2e.ts` — seeds one cold session, expands the sidebar group, clicks the row, renders history; then `/permission` chip, `/feedback`, compaction expand; zero model calls.
- `details-session-lifecycle.e2e.ts` — one replayed turn, drag sidebar handle, reload, then New session → original row → seeded row, asserting details track stays `0` across every ownership change.
- `lifecycle-chrome.e2e.ts` — first-send workspace+session materialization from the hero, reload recovery from the log (persisted `dsh.sessions.current`), dark-theme cascade; spins a second scaffold+page at :102-103 for plan mode.
- `sidebar-scrollbar.e2e.ts` — seeds many cold sessions (:391) purely to overflow the Sessions tree; hover in/out geometry.
- `sidebar-subagent-activity.e2e.ts` — pins a running descendant marker on its visible idle owner row.
- `cold-blank-session.e2e.ts` — seeds a blank session, asserts it stays out of the sidebar.

## 2. Multi-session fixture / replay support

`packages/test-support/llm-replay/src/index.ts`:
- `installLlmReplay` (:728) holds `bound = Map&lt;liveSessionId, {entries, cursor}&gt;` and `nextScript` (:739-740). A newly seen live session id claims the **next unclaimed script in bind order** (:743-767); cursors advance per session (:763).
- **Two concurrent streams do work mechanically** — pinned by `packages/test-support/llm-replay/tests/llm-replay.spec.ts:1065` ("routes each live session to its own script by FIRST-CALL order") and `:1079` ("keeps each session's cursor independent (interleaved calls)").
- The ordering is the gap. `loadSessionScripts` (:549) sorts children by `createdAt` then `recordedId` (:578), immediately above an explicit `// XXX(concurrent-subagents): concurrent children need an explicit first-call ordinal.` (index.ts:578). Which live session binds to which script is decided by whichever issues its first model call first — a race for two genuinely concurrent windows.
- Failure modes: unrecorded session (:750-760), script exhausted (:773-777), `assertConsumed` unbound-script/underrun (:786-800).

Scaffold wiring: `LaunchOptions.replayFixture` / `replayChildFixtures` / `replayOverride` / `replayProvidersOnly` / `paceMs` at `apps/web/tests/scaffold.ts:197-330`; install at :617-628; teardown `assertConsumed` at :677-685.

**Scaffold barrier limitation:** `whenTurnSettled` (`scaffold.ts:662-676`) listens for the first `turn/end` from **any** session — `ctx.on('session/event', (session, event) =&gt; { if (event.type !== 'turn/end') return; ... resolveSettled(session.id) })`. There is no session filter, so two concurrent turns cannot be awaited independently with it today.

Existing multi-fixture e2e (all parent/child subagent, driven sequentially): `subagent-conversation.e2e.ts:77-83`, `workflow-run.e2e.ts:54-55`, `subagent-interrupt-ui.e2e.ts:126-128`.

`FixtureApiClient` — `packages/client/connection/src/client/fixture.ts`:
- Three resident sessions at :1527-1531 (`fx-alpha` running, `fx-beta` child of alpha, `fx-gamma`).
- Per-session state maps: `logs` (:1532), `modelSelections` (:1533), `nextTurn` (:1558), `replays` `Map&lt;SessionId,…&gt;` with the comment "At most one in-flight replay per session" (:2022), `retryScenarios` (:2031).
- `mux` (:2927-2966): one `FxInbox` per connection; open baseline pushes `session/subscribed` + `session/projection` for **every running session** (:2934-2942), then pending approval/question frames hard-pinned to `fx-alpha` (:2947, :2957).
- `session.create` (:2353-2375) mints `fx-N`, emits `host/session-added`, optionally attaches a workspace; conflict path at :2336-2344. Full `session.*` routing table at :3349-3360.

## 3. Playwright interaction patterns available

**Drag** — `details-session-lifecycle.e2e.ts:104-113`: `boundingBox()` on `[data-side="sidebar"]`, then `page.mouse.move` / `.down()` / `.move(x+70, y, {steps:6})` / `.up()`, then `expect.poll(sidebarTrack) === before + 70`. This is the synthesis that works because the handles use React pointer events with `setPointerCapture` (`packages/client/ui-chat-surface/src/client/ChatSurface.tsx:48-72`). Its golden is *not* aria — `handleSnapshot()` (:46-65) reads every `[class*="handle"]` for `data-side`, computed `cursor`, and `::after` content into markdown; `apps/web/tests/snapshots/details-session-lifecycle/handles.expected.md` contains only a `## sidebar` block (the details handle is absent while collapsed).

Other drag: `navigation-panes.e2e.ts:389-392` (timeline overview interval drag + right-click clear).

**Hover synthesis**: `sidebar-scrollbar.e2e.ts:339-347` (`pointAt(page,'list'|'away')`), `markdown-wide-table.e2e.ts:388-396`, `chat-long-interactions.e2e.ts:103`, `chat-scroll-contract.e2e.ts:288`, `conversation-column-overflow.e2e.ts:132`, `cordis-tool-round.e2e.ts:176` (`mouse.move(0,0)` to park the cursor before a capture).

**Keyboard**: `markdown-wide-table.e2e.ts:358-370` (`.focus()` + `ArrowRight` scroll), `chat-scroll-contract.e2e.ts:759-769` (End/PageUp), `workflow-run.e2e.ts:103` (`Space` on a disclosure), `question-composer.e2e.ts:181` (Shift+Enter), `steering.e2e.ts:211/274/350` (Meta+Enter), plus many `Escape` dismissals. **There is no roving-tabindex or focus-order navigation spec.**

**Two browser pages**: `settings-chrome.e2e.ts:312, :371, :437, :471` (each against a second `launchWebScaffold`), `lifecycle-chrome.e2e.ts:102-103`, `markdown-wide-table.e2e.ts:418` (hidpi page).

**Reload / relaunch** — `desktop-recovery.e2e.ts` is the reference: `launch(extraOverlayPath?)` and `restart()` (:90-108) drive three Hosts over one shared `harnessHome` + `storageRoot`; `remembered(page)` reads `sessionStorage['dsh.desktop.id']` via `page.evaluate` (:126-133); `creations(scaffold)` collects `desktop/changed` on the settled ctx (:115-119); `?desktop=&lt;id&gt;` reopen (:172). Every reload spec pairs `page.reload({waitUntil:'load'})` with `acknowledgeReloadConnectionLoss` (`scaffold.ts:1020`) against the `watchConsole` tripwire (`scaffold.ts:1003`).

## 4. Client spec harness for session-runtime

`packages/client/session-runtime/tests/` (16 specs): `manager.client.spec.ts` (56 cases), `session.client.spec.ts` (50), `sessions-service.client.spec.ts` (39), `conversation-assembler` (25), `projection-store` (14), `queue-store` (14), `conversation-registry` (10), `partial` (9), `scope` (8), `lineage` (5), `tool-call-tree` (4), `client-apply.client.spec.ts` (3 — plugin browser-half apply: sessions service + Conversation registries mounting over the connection handle), `subagent-lineage` (2), plus `conversation`, `context-provenance`, `time-zone`.

The single-stage rule is stated in the module doc at `packages/client/session-runtime/src/client/service.ts:1-16`: *"the window opens ⟺ the session is on stage (today the stage is `current`; the staged state can widen to a multi-pane list later)"*. Implementation: `watched` field (:263-270), `followCurrent()` (:606-623, calls `record.session.open()` only for `list.current`), subscription at :315-318. `Session.open()` itself is idempotent (`session.ts:369-378`).

**Doubles** — `packages/test-support/client-runtime/src/sessions.ts`:
- `FixtureSession` (:25) implements `SessionFace`.
- `TestSessions` (:171) implements `ISessions`; owns `list: SnapshotStore&lt;SessionListState&gt;` (:173, initialized :202-205), `currentProvideInfo` from the **production** `SessionProvideChannel` (:206-217), a `records: Map&lt;SessionId, SessionRecord&gt;` (:180), and a `calls` log of `open | openSubagent | setSubagentCatalogOpen | refreshSubagents | clear | search | fork` (:185-190).
- **Multiple sessions: yes.** `add(fixture, {current?: false})` (:226-256) admits N sessions with one `current`; `updateSnapshot` (:262) is the per-session live-stream stand-in; `updateSummary` (:272); `setCurrent` (:285-292); `remove` prunes scope + per-session stores.
- **Event windows: no.** `open(id)` (:407-414) just writes `draft.current` + clears `currentAddress` synchronously; there is no staging or history pull in the double.
- `TestDesktop` (`packages/test-support/client-runtime/src/desktop.ts:25`) has `state: SnapshotStore&lt;DesktopViewState&gt;`, `set()` to install a whole view (:44), `setSurfaces` (:54), and **inert recording** `openWindow`/`closeWindow` (:72-88) — a spec that needs the mirror to move must write it with `set()`.

**ui-chat-\* / ui-conversation reliance on a "current" session:**
- `packages/client/ui-chat-surface/tests/chat-surface.client.spec.tsx` is the most coupled: a module-level mutable `selectedSession = {current}` (:23) feeds both the `SessionProviderStub` (:32-33) and the `useSessions` stub (:66-79), and ~10 cases mutate it (:121, :166, :188, :193, :199, :204, :207, :213, :218). Every case is written against one global selection.
- `ui-conversation` specs inject the id directly through a `SessionProvider` stub that ignores selection — `gate-branch-tails:44/130/187`, `skeleton:171/191/214/259`, `input-scenarios:139`, `input-bar:156`, `input-matrix:40`, `chat-view:264/282`. These survive removal of a global `current`.
- `ui-conversation/tests/apply-inject.client.spec.tsx:145, :264, :275, :290, :302, :308, :311` asserts `sessions.calls` contains `{method:'open', args:[ROOT|OTHER]}` — direct on `ISessions.open`. Its own header (:8-10) already records "History opening is NOT an inject concern — the runtime sessions service opens on watch".
- `ui-chat-sidebar/tests/sidebar-root.client.spec.tsx:34-76` runs its own `current` rerender harness over `useSessions`.

The new Window already takes the top zOrder, D8's open-in-new-window and D10's launcher would otherwise need a second CAS round trip the spec never mentions, and D4's "null when no windows" only holds this way.
- `closeWindow` **re-points focus** through the same fallback as minimize — forced by record integrity: `focusedWindowId` must never name a Window the aggregate dropped.

<result>W3 is complete. Everything left uncommitted and unstaged.

## Lease semantics as built

`ISessions.acquireView(sessionId): () =&gt; void` in `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/session-runtime/src/client/service.ts`:

- **State**: one `viewHolds: Map&lt;SessionId, number&gt;` where *an entry exists ⟺ the session is STAGED*, and the value counts live holds. A **zero value is the release grace**. Plus `releaseTimers: Map&lt;SessionId, Timeout&gt;`.
- **Acquire**: rejects unless `addressable(id)` (host-listed, or a durable child whose address `configureSubagentView` retained) — fail-loud like `open()` was. Cancels any pending sweep, increments; on the 0→1 edge `stageView` resolves the scope, `session.open()` (idempotent), `refreshSubagents(id)`, and `manager.stage(id)` which consumes the completion reminder. Further leases share that one staged session — no second `session.history`.
- **Release**: the returned disposer latches (repeat calls release once). Above 1 it decrements; the last one sets the count to 0 and schedules `setTimeout(sweep)` — a **macrotask grace**. Re-acquire inside the grace clears the timer and re-enters `stageView`, which no-ops on the already-open window. This is what makes StrictMode acquire/release/acquire and A-closes-while-B-opens handoffs leave the scope untouched.
- **Sweep**: deletes the entry, `manager.unstage(id)`, then `pruneScopes()`.
- **Eligibility**: `eligible = viewHolds.has(id) || ids.includes(id)` — the exact structural mirror of the old `current === id || ids.includes(id)`. So a session that leaves the list while staged keeps its scope frozen until its last lease releases (D1's "still tears down immediately via pruneScopes", preserving today's staged-defers rule). `deferredRemovals`/`sweepDeferred` are **deleted as redundant** — `pruneScopes` at every projection and every sweep covers them.
- Manager: `selected` → a `staged` Set driving completion reminders (`!staged.has(id)`), the session-added catalog refresh, and `handleConnected` (now iterates the staged set). `SessionListSnapshot`/`SessionListState` lost `current`/`currentAddress`; breadcrumb ancestor rows are now projected per staged addressed child.

**Three deviations from the brief**, each for a stated reason:
1. `configureSubagentView(parentSessionId, address)` takes a **required** address — the `| undefined` arm has no expressible target (there is no child id to clear) and no caller, so it would be uncovered fiction.
2. `workspace-runtime.startSession()` returns `Promise&lt;SessionId | undefined&gt;`, not `Promise&lt;SessionId&gt;` — `undefined` is the real "no Workspace exists to start one in" state that previously called `sessions.clear()`. Connect failures now propagate instead of being `console.warn`-swallowed.
3. `currentProvideInfo` removed per the pre-release stance, which also deletes `publishCurrent`/`resolveCurrent` from `SessionProvideChannel`. **W4 must supply ChatSurface a re-render trigger for late `sessions.provide()` registrations** — production has no republish channel now (I restored the equivalent in `TestSessions.provide` via `republishBinding`).

**One W1-surface correction** (`packages/client/ui-renderer/src/client/session-provider.tsx`): `SessionProvider` now provides its bundle to context in *both* branches, rendering `empty` as the body. Without it, `UNBOUND_SESSION_INFO`'s empty hook roster crashed any session-maybe occupant that renders session-less — `ConversationRoot` threw `useSession is not a function`. ui-renderer's 71 specs stay green.

## TODO(phase3-w4) production sites

| File | Site |
|---|---|
| `ui-workspace/src/client/index.ts:76,93` | `open` no-op, `boundSessionId: undefined`, fork no longer binds the child |

| `extensions/ui-cordis/src/client/CordisPanel.tsx:116` | Focused window's session unavailable; every row lists under "theirs" |

`WorkspaceBrowser` was not stubbed out: `boundSessionId` is now a real `WorkspaceBrowserInjected` member threaded through the tree derivations (`deriveGroups/deriveFlat/deriveSearchResults` gained an explicit `bound` parameter), so highlight/blank-row/auto-expand coverage survives intact and W4 wires one line. `ChatSurface` reads `owner.launchState.sessionId` for `detailsSession` (D5 truth, not a stub).

## Specs: rewritten vs mechanically fixed

**Rewritten** (behavior genuinely changed): `session-runtime/tests/sessions-service` (new *viewing leases* + *provide bundles* suites: shared staging, latching disposer, grace cancel, addressed-child lease lifecycle, fail-loud), `session-runtime/tests/manager` (staged-set reminders, `configureSubagentView`, reconnect), `workspace-runtime/tests/{workspaces-service,client-apply}` (startSession returns an id; the domain navigates nobody), `test-support/tests/runtime` (SessionBoundary frame + lease-recording case), `ui-conversation/tests/{apply-inject,assembly-surfaces}`, `ui-workspace/tests/{apply,rename-assembly,workspace-browser,tree}`, `ui-agent-preset/tests/apply`, `ui-workflow-run`, `ui-subagent/tests/browser-plugin`, `ui-settings-general/tests/settings-root`.

## Roster-republish mechanism

`SessionProvideChannel` gained a **roster revision feed** — the smallest honest signal, since the channel already re-mints bundles in place and only lacked a notification:

- `provide.ts`: private `version` + listener set; `applyRosterChange()` now materializes → rebuilds → **commits** → increments → notifies (so a roster that fails to materialize is never announced, and the rollback announces the restored one instead).
- `readonly roster: HostObservable&lt;number&gt;` on the channel, re-exposed as `ISessions.provideRoster` (`SessionRuntime` getter + `TestSessions` delegation to the same production channel).
- ChatSurface consumes it through its inject face's `hooks` compartment (`useProvideRoster`), then calls `provideInfo(boundId)` every render — the `views.subscribe/version` precedent, no new framework concept.

## Face shapes shipped

**Deviation from D7, with evidence:** the declaration-side children `inject` face is **not** per-instance — `cachedSlotInject(spec.inject)` in `ui-renderer/src/client/scoped-slots.tsx:136` caches by object identity in a `WeakMap`, and `spec.inject` is one static object per registration. Only `hooks` factories there receive per-dispatch context. So the per-window face rides **owner props** (declared by ui-chat-surface on its children, per-dispatch-site), with the exact D7 member naming preserved:

- `chat.sidebar` owner `+= { toggleSidebar, boundSessionId, openSession(id, opts?), startSession(wsId?, opts?) }`
- `chat.conversation` owner `= { layout: ChatLayoutFace, chatWindow: ChatWindowFace }`
- `chat.details` owner `= { layout }` (no navigation affordance, so no binding)
- `ChatLayoutFace = { openDetails, closeDetails }`; `ChatWindowFace = { boundSessionId, bind(id), openInNewWindow(id) }`; `ChatOpenOpts = { newWindow?: boolean }`
- ui-conversation forwards down its own subtree: `chatWindow` → session header + body; `openDetails`/`openSession` → view ring; `openSession` → chat-node seat and lineage seat.

Async navigation split accordingly: inject faces now perform the domain act and **return the Session** (`selectWorkspace`, `forkAt`, `forkSession`), and the component shows it in its own Window.

## Per-package changes

- **ui-chat-surface** — `service.ts` (LayoutController/ILayout/`ctx.layout`) deleted with its spec; new `ChatSurfaceInjected` face (provideRoster, provideInfo, acquireView, subagentAddress, configureSubagentView, startSession, openChatWindow); `inject = ['slots','sessions','workspaces','desktop']`; ChatSurface now binds from `launchState.sessionId`, holds one `acquireView` lease keyed on the binding, restores/persists `launchState.subagentAddress` (validated on read; a stale child id is ignored), auto-binds once from `recentWorkspaceId`, unbinds when the bound session leaves a settled list, and closes details on binding change. Added tsconfig references + deps for desktop-runtime/workspace-runtime/api-remotes.
- **ui-chat-sidebar** — inject drops to `['slots','locale']`, `SidebarRootInjected` deleted, bare register → `slots.inject('chat.sidebar', …)`, forwards the routing verbs into `chat.sidebar.workspaces`.
- **ui-conversation** — drops `'layout'`; every bare `slots.register` converted to `slots.inject` on its target; `DetailsInjected` retired; header `open` retired; owner shares added to session body/header/view/node/lineage.
- **ui-workspace** — `startSession`/`open`/`boundSessionId` moved from inject to the shell owner share (`open` → `openSession`); fork composed as fork-then-show; ctrl/cmd-click and a new **Open in new window** row-menu item (en+zh strings).
- **ui-workflow-run / ui-subagent** — both stop injecting navigation: run rows use the node owner's `openSession`; the subagent catalog is retain-then-show (`configureSubagentView`, then the lineage owner's `openSession`).
- **ui-agent-preset** — chip composes the Window's session via a new `compose(sessionId)` published from its render (`HeroAgentPresetOwnerProps.sessionId`, supplied by ConversationRoot's session-maybe `sessionId` — I kept the slot root-scoped rather than re-scoping it); `flowSessionId` deleted.
- **extensions/ui-cordis** — injects `desktop`; new `focusedSession` hook source derived from the desktop mirror; the panel groups by the focused Window's binding.
- **surface-chat** — stale launch-state doc comment corrected (version stays 1).

## Spec rewrites

Full rewrites: `ui-chat-surface/tests/chat-surface.client.spec.tsx` (owner-driven: bound/unbound, bind/rebind/new-window, lease acquire-release-handover, subagent restore + persist + stale-address rejection, auto-bind single-flight, unbind-on-removal, roster re-read, per-window stores) and `apply.client.spec.ts`. Updated: ui-chat-sidebar ×4, ui-conversation ×7 (incl. two new view-owner behavior cases in `skeleton`, two fork-and-show cases in `chat-view`), ui-workspace ×4, ui-subagent, ui-agent-preset ×2 (both previously-deferred assertions restored), ui-workflow-run, ui-tool ×7, ui-trajectory, session-runtime (two new roster cases), test-support.

Two things worth flagging: I removed 24 stray `tsc` emit artifacts (`.js`/`.d.ts`/`.map`) that had been left inside `packages/client/workspace-runtime/src/` — they shadowed the package entry and broke module loading in ~25 spec files. And I did **not** regenerate `slot-catalog.ts`/`api-catalog.ts` (they still describe `ctx.layout`); per the spec that regen is W8's. Web e2e was not run — W6 owns it.

## What I built (D10, all in `packages/client/ui-desktop`)

**New components**
- `chrome.ts` — every rule as a pure function: `showsTitlebar`, `drawnWindows`, `windowStyle`, `restorePlacement`, `cascadeBounds`, `movedBounds`, `resizedBounds`, `boundSessionOf`, plus `NARROW_DESKTOP`/`MIN_WINDOW_*`/`DEFAULT_FLOATING_BOUNDS`/`BOUND_SESSION_KEY`/`SESSION_SURFACE_ID`.
- `DesktopWindow.tsx` — the wrapper, capture-phase focus-on-pointerdown, and the local gesture rectangle.
- `WindowChrome.tsx` — `WindowTitlebar`, `WindowResizeCorner`, and one internal `PointerGesture`.
- `Taskbar.tsx` — ARIA toolbar, roving tabindex, StateDot, launcher `Menu`.
- `SessionNotices.tsx` — completion-transition watch, queue, and the Open action.
- Rewritten `DesktopFrame.tsx` (ResizeObserver + rAF narrow measurement, titles, notices wiring, `DocumentTitle`), `DesktopRoot.tsx` (the two multi-mutation sequences), `locales.ts` (17 new zh+en keys).

**Rules as shipped**
- **Titlebar**: `narrow → never`; else `mode === 'floating' || drawnWindows &gt; 1`. A lone full-screen Window keeps exactly its old wrapper — pinned by a test asserting the attribute set is exactly `class, data-desktop-surface, data-desktop-window, data-focused, style` with `style="z-index: 0;"` and one child.
- **Drag** only on floating (a full Window's title is plain text); **maximize** toggles `floating → full`, **restore** `full → floating`, bounds untouched. Resize clamps at 320×240. Both preview locally per rAF and send exactly one `updateWindow` at release; the preview is dropped when that Window's `revision` advances.
- **Placement on open**: first Window of a Desktop keeps `defaultPlacement`; every later one gets `{ mode: 'floating', bounds: (80+24k, 80+24k, 960, 640) }`, k = Windows already open, wrapping at 8. The opened Window is identified by diffing the mirror's ids across the awaited `openWindow` — so no `IDesktop` change was needed.
- **Restore** = `'floating'` if the record has bounds, else `'full'`; committed *before* focus (sequenced in `DesktopRoot`, because both mutations carry the revision held at send time and a parallel pair would conflict).
- **Narrow** (&lt;768, measured on `.desktop`'s own box): focused non-minimized Window only, full-bleed, no titlebars; falls back to the frontmost drawable Window when focus rests on a minimized one.

**Pointer-test technique** — mirrors `chat-surface.client.spec.tsx`: `installBrowserStubs()` in `tests/frame-bench.client.tsx` emulates `set/release/hasPointerCapture` per element via a `WeakSet`, runs rAF on the fake timer, and exposes a driveable `ResizeObserver` (`resizeFrameTo` / `resizeFrameWithoutFlush`). Gestures are real `PointerEvent`s with `pointerId`, dispatched inside `act`.

## Two decisions worth your review

1. **Notices are not the `Toast` primitive.** `Toast` renders text + an `aria-hidden` icon into a body portal with no action affordance, and the notice's entire point is the Open button; widening a statically-linked primitive for one consumer was outside W5. `SessionNotices` is `role="status"` cards in the overlay layer, hold 8s (documented as deliberately longer than Toast's 3s, since it must be reachable).
2. **`DocumentTitle` is a real cross-plugin value import.** `@deepseek-ai/dsh-client-ui-renderer/client` is now in ui-desktop's `dsh.client.external` (+ peer/dev deps, tsconfig ref). The built bundle's only `@deepseek-ai` requires are `ui-primitives` (platform baseline) and that one — purity gate green.

## Production bugs (left red, not papered over)

**1. A staged agent preset never reaches its Session.** `packages/client/ui-agent-preset/src/client/index.ts:167,171` stages `cordis` then calls `void scope.workspaces.startSession()`. The only trigger for `seat.apply()` besides `select()` is `index.ts:139` — `scope.sessions.list.subscribe(() =&gt; { void seat.apply() })`. `packages/client/workspace-runtime/src/client/service.ts:104-108` returns the already-bound blank Session id with no mutation and no publish, so no list notification fires and `apply()` never runs. Evidence: `agent-preset-authoring.e2e.ts:273` sees one session with `"agentPreset":"standard"`.

**2. Concurrent Window mutations are silently discarded.** `desktop-runtime/src/client/service.ts:213-222` reads `expectedRevision` at call time; `desktop-domain/src/index.ts:505` returns `{status:'conflict'}` *without applying*; `desktop-runtime/src/client/service.ts:231-241` then treats `conflict` exactly like `committed` — installs the snapshot, no retry, no warning. Callers (`DesktopRoot.tsx:69-76`, `ChatSurface.tsx:176`) are fire-and-forget, so the loss is invisible.

I hit this directly: **clicking Close on a non-focused Window drops the close.** `DesktopWindow.tsx:80` fires `focusWindow` from the wrapper's capture-phase pointerdown and the button's click fires `closeWindow` in the same tick with the same revision. A/B: my close-mid-stream test failed with the Window still open; adding a taskbar focus + settled wait before the close made it pass, nothing else changed.

`subagent-conversation.e2e.ts:497` is the same defect via a different door — a delegated code-reading diagnosis traced it to `ChatView.tsx:164-168` deferring `openSession(forkId)` until `forkAt` resolves (fork RPC *plus* a rename), so the fork's bind collides with the spec's next navigation and one is dropped.

That diagnosis also surfaced two latent defects not currently failing: `ChatSurface.tsx:218-223` calls `configureSubagentView` unconditionally on mount and `session-runtime/manager.ts:212-219` throws when the parent catalog is absent (always, on a fresh load) — so a reloaded Window bound to a subagent child throws into the slot error boundary; and `ui-subagent/src/client/index.ts:48-53` binds `openChild` to the slot's session id while the row builds its address from its own catalog owner, so opening a grandchild from an expanded branch throws at `manager.ts:215`.

**All four reported bugs verified, three of them wider than reported.** The subagent-navigation one (#4) is not just grandchildren: the injected `parentSessionId` is the *slot boundary's* session, while every row builds its address from its own catalog owner, so sibling switching and every ancestor breadcrumb throw too. The subagent-reload one (#3) additionally abdicates the shared registration — `abdicated` is a `WeakSet&lt;StoredEntry&gt;` keyed per *registration*, not per window instance, so one broken window drops **every** Chat window to the recovery tile. And the agent-preset one (#1) can also apply the preset to the wrong session and orphan the one it created.

**Two findings the workstream had not flagged, both blocker-class:**
- **D3 is dead code in production.** The whole per-window `instanceKey` pipeline exists (`ui-slots` RenderOpts, `scoped-slots` threading, `slots.ts:469` resolve, `slots.ts:304` `pruneStoreInstance`), but no production dispatch site passes `instanceKey` and nothing calls `pruneStoreInstance`. `DesktopFrame.tsx:235-250` omits it, so two Chat windows share one layout store — shared sidebar width, shared details column, shared narrow flag — which contradicts D6's per-window details requirement and the comment at `ui-chat-surface/src/client/index.ts:280-283` claiming per-instance stores.

Both sweeps are still running; I'll fold their results into the numbered report.</result>

# Residual findings — verified against current code

## MAJOR

**1. The settings creator-draft flow starts a Session no Window ever shows.**
`/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/ui-agent-preset/src/client/index.ts:166-180` now names the Session explicitly (`startSession().then(sessionId =&gt; seat.applyTo(sessionId))`), which fixes the staging half. But it still never binds. The comment at `:170-173` reasons only about the reuse branch ("reuse hands back a Session the list already carries"); the create branch in `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/workspace-runtime/src/client/service.ts:110-113` is equally reachable — whenever the Window is bound to a non-blank Session, or the recent Workspace holds no blank one. *Scenario:* a person reading a real conversation opens Settings, clicks the Creator add-card; the panel closes, a Session is minted and given the `cordis` preset, and the Window still shows the old conversation. The new Session is invisible until they find it in the sidebar. `packages/client/ui-agent-preset/README.md` still promises "stages it and starts a new session". *Remedy:* route it through the Window's own start-and-show verb (the `SidebarOwnerProps.startSession` owner share from D7/D8) rather than `ctx.workspaces.startSession()`, which by D6 performs no navigation.

**2. Auto-bind failure is permanent and silent — both the retry and the diagnostic were lost.**
`/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/ui-chat-surface/src/client/ChatSurface.tsx:244-249` sets `autoBound.current = true` *before* the attempt and never resets it; `:205-212` swallows the rejection behind a comment claiming "the workspace domain owns reporting why" — but `workspace-runtime/src/client/service.ts:126-130` → `connectWorkspace` → `sessions.create` only throws `SessionCreateError`, and nothing logs it anywhere. The retired code did both: `git show c94893a031:packages/client/workspace-runtime/src/client/service.ts` `startInitialSelection` reset `state = 'waiting'` so the next baseline projection retried, and logged `console.warn('initial workspace selection failed:', reason)`; its sibling `startSession` logged `console.warn('new session failed:', reason)`. *Scenario:* one transient create failure at startup strands that Window on the hero for the rest of the page load, with an empty console. *Remedy:* latch on success, not on attempt; log where the rejection is swallowed.

## MINOR

**3. The per-instance inject caches grow per retired Window; `pruneStoreInstance` does not reach them.** `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/ui-renderer/src/client/scoped-slots.tsx:99-101` holds three `WeakMap&lt;StoredEntry, Map&lt;string, InjectedProps&gt;&gt;` caches, and `instanceRow` (`:184-197`) only ever fills. `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/runtime/src/client/slots.ts:304-311` sweeps `this._stores` alone. Window ids are `window-&lt;uuid&gt;` and never recur, so every opened-then-closed Window leaves a permanent row. This became live only with the instanceKey wiring, and it is the half of D3's retirement contract that was missed. *Remedy:* add a renderer-host `pruneInstance(instanceKey)` dropping the row from all three caches, called from `SlotRegistry.pruneStoreInstance`.

**4. New Session no longer inherits the Window's own Workspace.** `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/workspace-runtime/src/client/service.ts:126-130` resolves `workspaceId ?? recentWorkspaceId`; the pre-phase-3 version resolved `workspaceId ?? currentWorkspaceId ?? recentWorkspaceId`. The `current` term was deleted without being replaced by the Window's `boundSessionId`, and no D-decision calls for the drop. *Scenario:* reading an older Session in W2 while W1 holds the newest Session — New Session starts in W1. *Remedy:* derive the term at the Chat frame (it already has `boundSessionId`) and pass it explicitly, or record the drop as intended.

**5. The Window limit fails silently.** `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/desktop-runtime/src/client/service.ts:250-252` reports every `rejected` with `console.warn` only; `window-limit` fires at `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/desktop/desktop-domain/src/index.ts:256-261` (12 by default). At the cap, "Open in new window", modifier-click, and the taskbar launcher all do nothing with no feedback. *Remedy:* surface refusals through the overlay layer that already carries `SessionNotices`.

**6. A launcher-opened second Chat Window binds the same blank Session as the first.** `Taskbar.tsx:109-112` opens with no launch state → `ChatSurface.tsx:245-249` auto-binds → `workspace-runtime/src/client/service.ts:104-109` reuse returns the blank Session the first Window already shows. The launcher is the only chrome affordance for "another window", and it produces two Windows on one Session — the opposite of the phase's headline capability. *Remedy:* create rather than reuse when another Window already binds the reuse candidate.

**7. Focusing a Window drops a drag preview mid-gesture.** `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/desktop/desktop-domain/src/index.ts:321-324` raises the Window's own `revision` on `focusWindow` though no placement changed; `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/ui-desktop/src/client/DesktopWindow.tsx:63` clears the preview on any `record.revision` change. Dragging an *unfocused* floating Window fires capture-phase focus at pointerdown (`:80`), whose answer lands mid-drag and snaps the Window back for one frame. The module doc at `:15-17` says the preview drops when the Host confirms "a change to this Window" — focus is not one. *Remedy:* drop the preview on a bounds/mode change, or leave `revision` untouched in `_focusWindow` (zOrder already moved).

**8. `DesktopRoot`'s module doc describes the mechanism the fix replaced.** `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/ui-desktop/src/client/DesktopRoot.tsx:6-10` still says "the sequences here await the first answer, which installs the revision the second one compares against", but `onSelectWindow` (`:88-91`) no longer awaits and ordering now comes from the service chain. *Remedy:* restate around the chain, keeping the one real reason `onOpenSurface` still awaits — it needs the new Window's id.

**9. The roster fan-out lost the containment its predecessor had.** `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/session-runtime/src/client/provide.ts:145-151` notifies with a bare loop; the deleted `publishCurrent` wrapped each call in try/catch with a stated reason. There is now one listener per mounted Chat Window, and `provide()` runs during plugin apply, so a throwing subscriber starves the rest and propagates into a registering plugin. *Remedy:* restore per-listener containment.

**10. `configureSubagentView`'s first parameter is dead.** `contract/sessions.ts:54`, `service.ts:377`, `manager.ts:242-246` — the guard can only fire if a caller passes something other than `address.parentSessionId`, and after the fix all three production sites pass exactly that (`ui-subagent/src/client/index.ts:59`, `ChatSurface.tsx:222`, forwarded verbatim at `ui-chat-surface/src/client/index.ts:258`). *Remedy:* take the address alone.

**11. Stale `duplicate-surface` prose in an English doc.** `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/docs/subsystems/desktop.md:17` still lists `duplicate-surface` in the result union and asserts "A Desktop holds at most one Window per Surface"; the union at `packages/desktop/desktop/src/types.ts:253-259` no longer carries the code, and D4 lifted the rule. The same file's "Implementations must honor" section *was* updated, so this is a missed paragraph, not the deferred zh mirror. (`packages/desktop/desktop/README.zh.md:30` repeats it and is the legitimate zh deferral.)

## INFO

**12. `ctrl+click` collides with the macOS secondary click.** `packages/client/ui-workspace/src/client/rows/Rows.tsx:33` accepts `metaKey || ctrlKey` on every platform; on macOS that gesture also raises the context menu.

**13. Entry abdication is registry-wide while entries now render per Window.** `packages/client/ui-slots/src/index.ts:1185-1191` abdicates by `StoredEntry` identity in a `WeakSet`, and `scoped-slots.tsx:762-764` abdicates for every non-chain kind. One `register()` call backs the `chat` Surface, so any single Window's ChatSurface crash retires the registration for *all* Windows, each then falling to the recovery tile (`DesktopFrame.tsx:249`) with no way back short of a plugin reload. The seeded subagent-reload bug was one route in; the rule outlives its fix and deserves a recorded decision rather than a code change now.

# Candidate areas that came up clean

- **notify-then-pull staleness** — an announcement during an in-flight resolve sets `needsRefresh` (`desktop-runtime/src/client/service.ts:168-172`) and the settling resolve starts exactly one more (`:93-107`), so an N+2 event during an N+1 pull is served; a self-caused event is dropped by `isBehind` (`manager.ts:87-90`).
- **`stampOpened` racing mutations** — both ride one per-Desktop tail (`desktop-domain/src/index.ts:374-379`, `:529-537`).
- **Reconnect unbinding every Window** — `session-runtime/src/client/manager.ts:436-467` merges rather than clears and holds `listPhase` at `ready`, so `boundListed` never transiently drops and `ChatSurface.tsx:232-237` cannot fire.
- **StrictMode lease thrash** — acquire clears the pending timer and refcounts; release parks the hold at `0` instead of deleting it, so `eligible()` stays true through the grace.
- **Lease held by a crashed Chat surface** — the boundary wraps the entry element (`scoped-slots.tsx:751,780`), so React unmounts ChatSurface and runs the lease effect's cleanup.
- **Focus reordering remounting Surfaces** — wrappers are keyed by `record.id`, so the zOrder re-sort reconciles by key; focusing one Window neither remounts nor unleases the other.
- **Narrow mode cancelling the hidden Window's work** — `chrome.ts:127-136` unmounts it and releases the lease, but the Session stays list-eligible so its scope and stream survive.
- **`document.title` races** — a single `DocumentTitle` at `DesktopFrame.tsx:314`, one effect writer.
- **Notices dedupe growth** — `SessionNotices.tsx:71-75` deletes the id as soon as `completed` clears.
- **Focus-fallback edges** — `refocus` (`desktop-domain/src/index.ts:118-128`) runs on close *and* update, so closing the last Window and minimizing every Window both land on `null`.
- **Cascade placement as a conflicting second mutation** — `DesktopRoot.tsx:105-117` awaits, and the chain serializes regardless.
- **`standardKit` growth on the instanceKey axis** — deliberately not keyed by instance; the instance reaches only `host.storeOf`, as the comment above `standardKit` states.
- **Cross-desktop leakage beyond `resolve`** — the wire surface is exactly six `@Remote` methods (`packages/desktop/desktop/src/index.ts:166-216`); `getSnapshot` is in-process. `listSurfaces` returns composition-global descriptors, `desktop/changed` carries `{desktopId, revision}`, and refusal messages name only caller-supplied ids. The remaining exposure — `resolve` serving any id to any caller — is already recorded at `packages/desktop/desktop/README.md:29`.

# Verdict

Must-fix before ship: **#1** and **#2** (user-visible dead ends that produce no diagnostic), and **#11** (a factually wrong English paragraph about a rule this phase lifted, which the docs-accompany-code rule makes an obligation, not a nicety). Should-fix in this phase because they are cheap and complete work already started: **#3** (finishes D3's retirement contract), **#8**, **#9**, **#10**. The rest are follow-ups; **#13** wants a recorded decision rather than code.

I did not run an official build or the three e2e lanes in replay — I skipped the build deliberately, so the e2e evidence in this review is static only.

result: Phase 3 review complete — 13 residual findings (2 MAJOR, 9 MINOR, 2 INFO) verified against the post-fix tree with typecheck, the cordis-API gate, and 4259 client/desktop tests green; must-fix before ship is the creator-draft flow that starts an unshown Session, the permanently-latched silent auto-bind failure, and the stale duplicate-surface paragraph in docs/subsystems/desktop.md:17.</result>

`paceMs` waits per replayed chunk before yielding. Fixture chunk counts: A = 7 / 46 / 46, B = 32 / 32 / 46.

- **Live open (remedy 3):** B's third turn is 46 chunks = 13.8 s; the three mid-stream pointer events cost &lt;1 s, leaving ~12.8 s.

A `notices.count()===1` taken straight after brackets the whole capture inside the notice's life — dismissal is one-way, so bracketing is proof, not just margin.

## 2. The current Surface app template

**Host half** — `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/desktop/surface-chat/src/index.ts`. Whole package is 51 lines: `PACKAGE_NAME`, `CHAT_LAUNCH_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1` (`:26`), `name = 'surface-chat'`, `inject = ['desktop']`, and `apply` wrapping one `ctx.desktop.registerSurface({ id: SurfaceId('chat'), pluginName, label: 'Chat', launchSchemaVersion, launchSchema: windowLaunchState, defaultLaunchState: {}, defaultPlacement: 'full' })` in `ctx.effect(..., 'surface-chat: chat surface definition')` (`:39-50`). `SurfaceDefinition` is declared at `packages/desktop/desktop/src/index.ts:71-93`; `defaultPlacement` is typed `'full'` only. `packages/desktop/surface-chat/package.json` has no `dependencies` — `dsh-desktop`, `dsh-invariants`, `cordis` are peer+dev only. Its spec (`tests/surface-chat.spec.ts`) mounts a `StubDesktopService extends DesktopService` rather than the real provider, and asserts identity, every definition field, `launchSchema.safeParse` behavior, and fiber-scoped withdrawal/re-registration.

**Client half, post-phase-3** — `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/ui-chat-surface/src/client/index.ts`. The registration block is `:286-306`:
```ts
ctx.slots.inject('desktop.window.surface', () =&gt; ctx.slots.register({
  name: 'desktop.window.surface',
  key: 'chat',
  meta: { launchSchemaVersion: 1 },
  children: {
    'chat.sidebar': { kind: 'single', scope: 'root' },
    'chat.conversation': { kind: 'single', scope: 'session-maybe' },
    'chat.details': { kind: 'single', scope: 'session' },
  },
  store: createLayoutStore,
  inject: (): ChatSurfaceInjected =&gt; face,
}, ChatSurface))
```
`CHAT_SURFACE_ID` exists at `:49` but the registration repeats the literal `'chat'` — the comment there says the client-catalog generator reads registration text from source and resolves no constants (same rule at `packages/client/ui-desktop/src/client/index.ts:129-131`). `inject = ['slots', 'sessions', 'workspaces', 'desktop']` (`:245`). `face` is built once at `:271-285` because it is Window-independent; per-window differentiation is the `store` factory (`store: createLayoutStore` — the factory itself, not an instance) and the `instanceKey` the frame passes. The three child slots are declared in the `SlotMap` augmentation at `:51-94`; the owner-share contracts (`ChatLayoutFace`, `ChatWindowFace`, `SidebarOwnerProps`, `ConvOwnerProps`, `DetailsOwnerProps`, `ChatSurfaceInjected`) are exported from the same file at `:103-242`.

**Per-window instance stores.** The frame side: `packages/client/ui-desktop/src/client/DesktopFrame.tsx:235-255` passes `entryKey: record.surfaceId`, `instanceKey: record.id`, `fallback: tile('unavailable')`. Retirement: `packages/client/ui-desktop/src/client/DesktopRoot.tsx:133-143` holds a `useRef&lt;ReadonlySet&lt;string&gt;&gt;` of window ids and calls `ctx.slots.pruneStoreInstance(windowId)` for each departed one, gated on `view.phase === 'ready'`. Runtime implementation at `packages/client/runtime/src/client/slots.ts:306-314` (`pruneStoreInstance`) and `:462-482` (`resolveStore`, key = `record.scope === 'root' ? instanceKey ?? ROOT_INSTANCE_KEY : sessionId`).

**Launcher surface catalog — automatic.** `packages/client/ui-desktop/src/client/Taskbar.tsx:81-114`: `items` is `surfaces.map(surface =&gt; ({ id: surface.id, label: surface.label }))` fed into `Menu`, with `t('taskbar.launcher.empty')` when the catalog is empty; `onSelect` calls `onOpen(id)`. `surfaces` arrives from `DesktopRoot.tsx:150` (`surfaces={ctx.desktop.surfaces()}`) → `DesktopFrame.tsx:309`. `ctx.desktop.surfaces()` is the mirror's cached `listSurfaces` result, refreshed on `desktop/surfaces-changed` (`packages/client/desktop-runtime/src/client/index.ts:43`). So a Host `registerSurface` call is all it takes for a new entry to appear in the launcher — no client-side list. Placement of the opened window is `DesktopRoot.tsx:104-118` (`onOpenSurface`): first Window of a Desktop keeps the Surface default, every one after floats at `cascadeBounds(before.length)`.

**Launch-state binding, the pattern a Notes surface mirrors for `noteId`.** In `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/ui-chat-surface/src/client/ChatSurface.tsx`:
- key constant `:37` — `const SESSION_KEY = 'sessionId'`
- exported reader `:50-53`:
  ```ts
  export function boundSessionOf(launchState: Readonly&lt;Record&lt;string, unknown&gt;&gt;): SessionId | undefined {
    const stored = launchState[SESSION_KEY]
    return typeof stored === 'string' ? stored as SessionId : undefined
  }
  ```
- read site `:157` — `const boundSessionId = boundSessionOf(launchState)`
- commit path `:176-190` — the `bind` callback; comment at `:172-175` states the loop ("Writing the binding IS the navigation: the Host stores the launch state, the mirror republishes it, and this frame re-renders against the new `launchState`"):
  ```ts
  const bind = useCallback((sessionId: SessionId) =&gt; {
    const address = subagentAddress(sessionId)
    const { [SUBAGENT_KEY]: _stale, ...rest } = launchState
    chatWindowControls.update({ ...rest, [SESSION_KEY]: sessionId, ... })
  }, [chatWindowControls, launchState, subagentAddress])
  ```
- unbind path `:260-265` — destructure the keys out of `launchState` and `chatWindowControls.update(rest)`.
- `window.update` is the owner share declared at `packages/client/ui-desktop/src/client/index.ts:99-112` and supplied at `DesktopFrame.tsx:242` → `DesktopRoot.tsx:75-77` → `ctx.desktop.updateWindow(windowId, { launchState })`.
- The same reader is duplicated in the plugin body for cross-window reads (`src/client/index.ts:262-270`, `shownElsewhere`) and mirrored in the e2e helper (`apps/web/tests/desktop-windows.ts:212-215`).

**Recovery gate.** `DesktopFrame.tsx:232-234` — a registrant whose `meta.launchSchemaVersion` differs from `record.launchSchemaVersion` renders `RecoveryTile` with reason `'incompatible'`; `readSurfaceHealth` at `DesktopRoot.tsx:47-57` resolves `meta` off `ctx.slots.entriesOfSlot('desktop.window.surface')` and `pluginName`/`label` off `ctx.desktop.surfaces()`.

## 3. Markdown display/editing substrate

**Markdown lives in `ui-primitives`, not in `ui-conversation`** — so it is already reusable by a fresh package with no conversation dependency. `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/client/ui-primitives/src/markdown/` holds `MarkdownText.tsx`, `MessageText.tsx`, `CodeBlock.tsx`, `JsonBlock.tsx`, `parse.ts`, `incremental.ts`, `render.tsx`, `highlight.ts`, `katex.tsx`, `plain-text.ts`, `cjkFriendlyStrong.ts`, `mathCompatibility.ts`. Exported at `packages/client/ui-primitives/src/index.ts:46-53`. `ui-primitives` is cordis-free (its only peers are `dsh-invariants` and `cordis`), takes localized copy as props (`CodeBlock.tsx:20-24` `copyLabel`/`copiedLabel`), and exports only `.`, `./invariant`, `./src/*`.

- `MarkdownText` (`MarkdownText.tsx:1-26`) — mdast pipeline (`mdast-util-from-markdown` + `micromark-*` + `micromark-extension-gfm`/`-math`), incremental streaming parser that freezes all but the trailing two blocks, props `text`, `streaming`, `codeLabels: MarkdownCodeLabels`, `fileMentions: MarkdownFileMentions`. Imports `katex/dist/katex.min.css`.
- Shiki: `highlight.ts:21-25` — `createHighlighterCoreSync` + `createJavaScriptRegexEngine` (no WASM), three boot grammars (typescript/shellscript/json) plus a lazy set; theme is `createCssVariablesTheme`, colors are `--shiki-*` tokens owned by `ui-theme`.
- `MessageText` (`MessageText.tsx`, 7 lines) — literal-text primitive for user/steering content.
- Consumers today: `ui-conversation/src/client/chat/AssistantMarkdown.tsx:15,56`, `CompactionItem.tsx:76`, `MessageItem.tsx:182,208`, `skeleton/DetailsPanel.tsx:105`; also `ui-trajectory/src/client/TrajectoryTable.tsx:1044,1557,2842`, `ui-user-questions/src/client/{QuestionComposer,PlanReviewPanel}.tsx`. `AssistantMarkdown` itself is conversation-private (it takes `AssistantBlock[]` and `ChatViewSlotProps['t']`).

**No markdown editor exists.** Text editing today:
- The composer textarea — `packages/client/ui-conversation/src/client/skeleton/InputBar.tsx:737` (a raw `&lt;textarea&gt;` with a mirror-layer backdrop for chip decorations, auto-grow, `beforeinput` handling; `:722-745`). `packages/client/ui-primitives/src/Input.tsx:1-2` explicitly says composer textareas are **not** the `Input` atom and live with the conversation package.
- Other textareas: `ui-user-questions/src/client/QuestionComposer.tsx:88` (auto-growing free-text answer with a shared-metrics mirror), `ui-message-feedback/src/client/MessageFeedbackActions.tsx:296`.
- Settings are hand-rolled `&lt;input&gt;` elements, not the `Input` atom: `ui-settings-plugins/src/client/fields.tsx:72,111` (`ValueField`/`ToggleField`, staged-draft + `overridden`/`invalid`/reset shape); `ui-settings-models/src/client/ProviderEditor.tsx:375,400,422,443`. No `&lt;textarea&gt;` anywhere in `ui-settings*`.

**ui-primitives inventory** (`src/index.ts`): `StateDot`+`StateDotState`, `DisclosureRow`, `Button`+`ButtonVariant`, `Pill`, `Input`, `Menu`+`MenuEntry|MenuItem|MenuSeparator|MenuLabel`, `HoverCard`, `Modal`, `OnboardingSurface`, `RiskConfirmation`, `ConnectionBanner`, `FishLogo`, `BrandWordmark`, `Tooltip`+`TooltipSide`, `Toast`, `writeClipboard`, `JsonTree`, `TerminalBlock`, `ReadBlock`, `DiffBlock`, `SearchBlock`, `WebBlock`, `CodeBlock`, `JsonBlock`, `MarkdownText`, `MessageText`, `extractMarkdownPlainText`; hooks `useAnchoredMaxHeight`, `useAnchoredPosition`, `useDismissOnOutsidePointer`; plus `export * from './icons/index.tsx'` (71 exports).

**CSS modules + tokens in a fresh client package** — `RecoveryTile` as the small template: `packages/client/ui-desktop/src/client/RecoveryTile.tsx` (`import css from './RecoveryTile.module.css'`, `className={css.tile}`, copy via `t: TranslateNS&lt;'desktop'&gt;`, pure component) + `RecoveryTile.module.css` (only `var(--dsw-alias-*)` — `bg-base`, `label-primary/secondary/tertiary`, `border-l2`, `interactive-bg-hover`; no literal colors). The package needs `src/css-modules.d.ts` (`declare module '*.module.css'` → `Record&lt;string,string&gt;`; `declare module '*.css'`) and `tsdown.config.ts` = `clientBundle('&lt;pkg&gt;', ['lib/types/index.js', 'lib/types/invariant.js'])` from `../tsdown.client.ts`.

## 5. storage-domain provider template — confirmations

- **`defineDomain` versioning** — `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/storage/storage-domain/src/spec.ts:79-98`: identity helper that validates at module load — `UNIT_NAME_RE` on the domain name and each table name, `version` must be a non-negative integer, and a `global.schema` accepting `null` throws (null is the medium's "never written" sentinel). `descriptorOf` `:105-113` projects `{ name, version, tables, hasGlobal }`. Confirmed: version bump is a hard gate — `DomainSpec.version` doc at `:39` says "a medium stamped with a different version rejects at open."
- **Spec file placement** — `packages/desktop/desktop-domain/src/spec.ts` is 23 lines: import the record schema from the *Definition* package, `defineDomain({ name: 'desktop', version: 0, tables: { desktops: domainTable&lt;DesktopId, DesktopSnapshot&gt;(desktopSnapshot) } })`, re-exported from `src/index.ts:21`. The comment `:12-15` records why one table of whole aggregates: the domain form has no cross-table transaction, so one record + one `put` is the commit point.
- **Per-key tails** — `packages/desktop/desktop-domain/src/index.ts:184` (`operationTails: Map&lt;DesktopId, Promise&lt;void&gt;&gt;`) and `:529-540` (`enqueue`, with self-cleanup `if (this.operationTails.get(desktopId) === tail) delete`). The header comment `:151-156` notes that *every* write — mutations, the creating put, and the `lastOpenedAt` stamp — rides the same queue.
- **Publish-at-commit invariant placement** — on the **Definition** package (`packages/desktop/desktop/src/invariant.ts`), with `inject: ['desktop']` attached via `Object.assign`, so it holds for every provider. The provider's own `packages/desktop/desktop-domain/src/invariant.ts` is a justified empty with a `No runtime invariant:` paragraph explaining that the queue/idempotency/CAS decisions are synchronous private state a companion cannot observe.
- **MemoryStorageBackend** — `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/storage/storage-domain/tests/helpers/memory-backend.ts:117` (`MemoryStorageBackend`) and `MemoryMediaPool` (`:29`, shared across instances to simulate reopening the same medium). Imported by relative path from other packages' tests, e.g. `packages/desktop/desktop-domain/tests/helpers/harness.ts:17-20` and `packages/automation/automation-domain/tests/loader-composition.spec.ts:19`.
- **Loader-composition test shape** — `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/desktop/desktop-domain/tests/loader-composition.spec.ts:33-58` (`bootFromYml`): mkdtemp, write `cordis.yml`, `new Context()` + `baseUrl`, `plugin(Storage)`, `storage.backend.register('memory', new MemoryStorageBackend(new MemoryMediaPool()))`, `provide('storageDomain', new DomainFacility(booted, { backend: 'memory', routes: {} }))`, `plugin(Loader)`, `loader.builtins.include = Include`, a stubbed `loader.internal.import` answering only the package under test, `loader.create({ name: 'cordis:include', config: { path } })`, `loader.await()`. Shared harness at `tests/helpers/harness.ts` (real `Storage` + `DomainFacility` over the memory medium, `surfaceDefinition()` factory, `Changed`/`SurfaceChange` capture types that record what a listener could read back *while the event dispatches*).
- The `storage-domain` row itself is a **web-platform** bundle row (`packages/bundle/web-platform/cordis.patch.yml:53-56`, `config: { backend: json }`, `routes` left default) — nothing per-domain to register there.

## 1. The tool-package recipe as `packages/automation/tool-automation` implements it

**Package layout** (`packages/automation/tool-automation/`): `src/index.ts` (587 lines, single file — all three tools), `src/invariant.ts`, `tests/tool-automation.spec.ts`, `package.json`, `tsconfig.json`, `README.md`, `README.zh.md`, `README.i18n.yaml`. No `types.ts`, no `client.ts`.

**Plugin form** — function plugin, no default export (`packages/CLAUDE.md` rule):
- `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/automation/tool-automation/src/index.ts:20-21` — `export const name = 'tool-automation'`, `export const inject = ['tools', 'automations']`.
- `:274-277` — `export function apply(ctx: Context, config: Config): void`, then `const bounds = config as Required&lt;Config&gt;` because the exported `Config` schema already filled every default.

**Registration is effect-scoped**: `ctx.tools.register(defineTool({...}))` at `:279`, `:455`, `:511`. The registry implements this as a layer effect on the *calling* context — `/Users/ren/repos/deepseek-harness/packages/core/tools/src/index.ts:1057-1061` (`this.layers.effect(this.ctx, layer =&gt; layer.tools.insert(name, definition), { label: 'tools.register()' })`). A plain plugin context registers globally; `agent.ctx` registers for one agent. Disposing the fiber withdraws the tools (proven at `tests/tool-automation.spec.ts:200-211`).

**Schema library**: the `dsh-tools` in-house DSL, not zod. `parameters` is a `ParameterSchemaSpec` record (implicit open object root); `output.schema` is a `ValueSchemaSpec` (any JSON root). `defineTool` validates model arguments before `execute` and infers `args`/return types (`docs/cookbook/adding-a-tool.md:42`). Schemastery (`z`) is used *only* for the plugin `Config` (`src/index.ts:9,38-41`). Where a domain record already has a zod schema, the tool re-parses through it rather than duplicating rules: `:429-442` calls `automationRuleInput.safeParse(...)` and rethrows issue paths as `invalid automation rule: &lt;path&gt;: &lt;message&gt;`.

Constraints the DSL cannot express are hand-checked in helpers that throw plain `Error`: `:147-152` `validateRuleId` (non-empty), `:159-164` `validateLimit` (≥1), `:561-570` the cross-field `kill_switch` ⊕ `rule_id` rule.

**Descriptions** are module constants, not inline: `:98-113 CREATE_DESCRIPTION`, `:115-122 LIST_DESCRIPTION`, `:124-129 SET_ENABLED_DESCRIPTION`. `CREATE_DESCRIPTION` interpolates `AUTOMATION_EVENT_NAME_PATTERN.source` at `:103` so the prompt cannot drift from the validator (pinned by the test at `tests/tool-automation.spec.ts:213-218`).

**Output declaration**: `output: { schema, render }` — `:409-425`, `:472-488`, `:529-559` (the last uses a `oneOf` of two closed objects discriminated by a `const` `scope`). `render: (_args, value) =&gt; [{ type: 'text', text: ... }]` is a pure projection; the prose builders are separate module functions: `:209-213 renderDispatchState`, `:222-231 renderRules`, `:239-246 renderRuns`, `:253-257 renderAutomationState`. Record→listing projections: `:173-184 listedRule`, `:191-201 listedRun` (a zero-valued diagnostic counter is omitted rather than emitted).

**Render intent declaration**: all three tools return the *generic* card, via one shared helper — `:265-267`:
```ts
function presentAutomationCall(title: string, rawInput?: unknown): GenericCallView {
  return { card: 'generic', title, kind: 'other', ...rawInput === undefined ? {} : { rawInput } }
}
```
`presentCall` at `:452`, `:508`, `:580-585`. No `presentResult`, no `presentationMeta`, no `locations` — the tools touch no file. Per `docs/cookbook/adding-a-tool.md:71-90` the three call cards are `generic` (with optional `kind` icon and `locations: [{ path, line? }]`), `terminal` (tool-bash), and `diff` (tool-fs write/edit); result cards add `search`, `read`, `web`. The hard rules at `:86-88`: **purity** (presenters run on live streaming *and* session-log replay — no I/O, no session state, no clock/random), **UI-only formatting stays out of the model result**, and `defineTool` soft-validates the display path so malformed older logged args return `undefined` (generic fallback) instead of crashing replay. `locations` is what a capable editor follows — a desktop/notes tool has no file path, so `generic` is the correct card, matching automation.

**Presentation as a pure function of args** is directly asserted: `tests/tool-automation.spec.ts:513-520` and `:600-613` call `ctx.tools.get('automation_list')?.presentCall?.({...})` and compare the exact object.

**Config bounds pattern**: `src/index.ts:24-41`

```ts
export interface Config { maxListedRules?: number; maxListedRuns?: number }
export const Config: z&lt;Config&gt; = z.object({

  maxListedRuns: z.number().step(1).min(1).default(20),

```
Each field carries a JSDoc justifying the number ("Twenty rules fit a listing the model can act on without spending a turn's context on inventory"). Tests: `:233-237` (defaults + rejection of `0` / `1.5`), `:239-246` (a row configuring nothing still gets 20), `:496-511` (larger request reduced, smaller honored).
